Theorem 217.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: In the general framework, every convergence analysis morphism f: X → Y extends to a continuous map on the completion with Lipschitz constant 654. The resulting operator achieves bound O(345/n^2).

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the general space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 654 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 345. QED.

The operator on the ambient space provides the required extension, and the obstruction class in H^706(S, F) is trivial. QED.
